#### [3. Awesome Gemini Cli](/content/Piebald-AI/awesome-gemini-cli/README.md)

##### Commands & Extensions

* [AsItIs (⭐4)](https://github.com/Katta041/asitis-agent-tools) ⭐ 4 | 🐛 7 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-18 - Gemini CLI extension and CLI that lints GEMINI.md, AGENTS.md and SKILL.md. Shows which files Gemini CLI loads at startup and about how many tokens they cost, and flags instructions hidden in comments or invisible characters. Read-only, runs offline.

##### Development Tools & Utilities

* [Folkbench Switch](https://folkbench.com/en/switch?utm_source=github\&utm_medium=awesome\&utm_campaign=awesome-gemini-cli) - Open-source desktop app (Tauri/Rust) that saves API provider profiles and writes the selected endpoint and key into Gemini CLI's `~/.gemini/.env` and `settings.json`, with a diff preview before writing. Also handles Claude Code and Codex.
* [Buddy Sandboxes](https://buddy.works/docs/ai-agents/sandboxes) - Isolated Ubuntu machines in the Buddy.Works cloud for running Gemini CLI away from your laptop. Install it in the sandbox with `npm install -g @google/gemini-cli`, connect over SSH, and the agent can install packages and run commands without touching your machine. The `bdy sandbox` CLI also copies files, exposes ports on public endpoints and takes snapshots.

##### Browser Extensions

* [Open in AI Agent for Gmail](https://openinaiagent.com/?utm_source=awesome-gemini-cli\&utm_medium=referral) - Open-source Chrome, Edge and Firefox extension ([GitHub (⭐1)](https://github.com/thomasoclegg/gmail-to-agent) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 JavaScript | 📅 2026-11-17) that right-clicks single or multiple Gmail threads directly into separate named `gemini -i` or Antigravity (`agy`) sessions in your working folder, plus unread inbox triage up to 25 threads.

##### MCP Servers

* [zaDojo (⭐2)](https://github.com/zaDojo/seo-geo-mcp) ⭐ 2 | 🐛 0 | 📅 2026-11-17 - Hosted SEO and GEO MCP server: free Google Search Console, Google Analytics and site audits, plus pay-as-you-go SERP and keyword research, rank tracking and AI visibility tracking in ChatGPT, Gemini, Perplexity and Google AI Overviews. Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth sign-in or an API key. Add with `gemini mcp add --transport http --scope user zadojo https://app.zadojo.com/mcp`, then `/mcp auth zadojo`. [Gemini CLI guide](https://zadojo.com/docs/gemini-cli).
* [Agent Nexus (⭐1)](https://github.com/Strategiumfinancepartner/AgentNexus) ⭐ 1 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 TypeScript | 📅 2026-11-17 - Continuous discovery registry and live health observability for 730+ remote MCP servers and APIs with tool schema validation and split-fleet detection. Free tier with 1,000 requests/day. Remote Streamable HTTP endpoint: <https://agentnexus.app/mcp>.
* [CallForMe](https://callforme.tel/?ref=awesome-gemini-cli) - Hosted MCP server that lets Gemini CLI phone a business for you: it works the phone menu, waits on hold, asks your agent mid-call when the business needs something and returns answers plus a transcript. Remote Streamable HTTP with OAuth. Pay per call, $1 per answered call; demo line with no account.

* [AutoResearch](https://autoresearch.me/en/): Browser-based AI research system that connects literature exploration, research ideas, model implementation, GPU experiments and manuscript drafting. Private beta: apply on the site, wait for administrator approval, then set a password from the follow-up email and sign in; approved members receive 5,000 starter credits. Official pages say access is free during the beta, but this is a limited allowance, not unlimited use and not access without an account. Prices for future plans are not yet announced.

#### [8. Awesome Computational Biology](/content/inoue0426/awesome-computational-biology/README.md)

##### Drug Response & Perturbation

* [scCT-DB](http://scctdb.ncpsb.org.cn) — Cancer treatment single-cell database with 266 paired pre/post-treatment datasets, 6.19M cells, 1,142 samples, 27 cancer types, and 102 drugs.
* [scICB](http://www.scimmnue.com/) — Pan-cancer ICB therapy single-cell database spanning 338 patients, 807 samples, 3.69M cells, and 13 cancer types, including 247 matched pre/post-treatment patients.
* [CellResDB](https://cellknowledge.com.cn/cellresponse) — Cancer therapy response single-cell database containing 4.7M cells from 1,391 patient samples, 72 datasets, and 24 cancer types.
* [Gondal et al. 2025 ICB scRNA-seq collection](https://cellxgene.cziscience.com/collections/61e422dd-c9cd-460e-9b91-72d9517348ef) — Integrated ICB-treated cancer single-cell collection from eight studies and 223 patients, with 90,270 cancer cells and 265,671 other cells.

##### Genetic Perturbation & Perturb-seq

* [scPerturBench (BM2 Lab) (⭐110)](https://github.com/bm2-lab/scPerturBench) ⭐ 111 | 🐛 5 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-10-22 — Benchmark comparing 27 single-cell perturbation-response prediction methods across 29 genetic and chemical perturbation datasets.
* [scPerturbBench (TianGzlab / PerturbArena) (⭐11)](https://github.com/TianGzlab/scPerturbBench) ⭐ 11 | 🐛 0 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-06-12 — Benchmark of single-cell genetic perturbation prediction covering unseen perturbations, combinations, cell-state transfer, and 24 evaluation metrics.

##### General Biological ML & Knowledge Graph Benchmarks

* [CellBench (CellVoyager) (⭐273)](https://github.com/zou-group/CellVoyager/tree/main/CellBench) ⭐ 275 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-06-02 — Benchmark for evaluating LLMs and data-analysis agents on reproducible single-cell research tasks.

##### Preprocessing Tools

* [SpatialData](https://spatialdata.scverse.org/) — Interoperable data structures and tooling for spatial omics, microscopy images, coordinates, segmentation shapes, and annotations.

##### Bioinformatics & Omics Agents

* [CellVoyager (⭐273)](https://github.com/zou-group/CellVoyager) ⭐ 275 | 🐛 8 | 🌐 Python | 📅 2026-06-02 — Jupyter-based scientific agent that plans, executes, and evaluates single-cell transcriptomics analyses, accompanied by CellBench.

##### Single-cell Foundation Models / Transcriptomics Foundation Models

* [Cell2Sentence / C2S-Scale (⭐896)](https://github.com/vandijklab/cell2sentence) ⭐ 896 | 🐛 14 | 🌐 Jupyter Notebook | 📅 2026-10-06 — Single-cell language-model family representing gene-expression profiles as cell sentences for cellular interpretation, generation, and question answering.

##### Multi-Modal Foundation Models / Protein Structure Prediction and Design

* [TxGemma](https://deepmind.google/models/gemma/txgemma/) — Open Gemma-based therapeutic-development model family (2B, 9B, and 27B) for property prediction and interactive scientific reasoning.
* [NVIDIA Nemotron for Science](https://nvidianews.nvidia.com/news/nvidia-introduces-nemotron-for-science-to-accelerate-discoveries-with-agents) — Scientific-agent development stack based on Nemotron 3.5 Super VL, with post-training recipes and chemistry reasoning benchmark LitmusBench v0.2.

#### [18. Free for Dev](/content/ripienaar/free-for-dev/README.md)

##### Tools for Teams and Collaboration

* [Notion](https://www.notion.com/) - Notion is a note-taking and collaboration application with markdown support that integrates tasks, wikis, and databases. The company describes the app as an all-in-one workspace for note-taking, project management and task management. In addition to cross-platform apps, it can be accessed via most web browsers.
* [zoom](https://zoom.com/) - Secure Video and Web conferencing add-ons available. The free plan is limited to 40 minutes.

##### Email

* [addy.io](https://addy.io) - Open-source anonymous email forwarding, create unlimited email aliases for free

##### CDN and Protection

* [ovh](https://www.ovhcloud.com/en-ie/security/ssl-gateway/) - Free DDoS protection and SSL certificate

##### Managed Data Services

* [Raff](https://rafftechnologies.com/products/managed-databases) - Managed PostgreSQL, MySQL and Valkey, 1 free database per account with no expiry, 1 vCPU, 1 GB RAM and 2 GB storage (Valkey: 512 MB memory), 20 direct and 200 pooled connections on PostgreSQL, TLS on every database, pauses after 7 days without connections and resumes in about a minute. No egress fees.

##### IDE and Code Editing

* [Eclipse Che](https://www.eclipse.dev/che/) - Web-based and Kubernetes-Native IDE for Developer Teams with multi-language support. Open Source and community-driven. An online instance hosted by Red Hat is available at [workspaces.openshift.com](https://workspaces.openshift.com/).
* [OnlineCompiler.io](https://onlinecompiler.io/) - Online compiler and code execution API for 46 languages. The editor is free with no sign-in, and the free API plan includes 100,000 runs a month.
